Heavy work in the decorator body slows startup and is easy to miss."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Order matters with method decorators."}]," ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@property"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@classmethod"}],", and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@staticmethod"}]," go outermost, your decorator goes closest to ",["$","code",null,{"children":"def"}],"."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"A cached method leaks."}]," ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@lru_cache"}]," on a method puts ",["$","code",null,{"children":"self"}]," in the key and keeps the instance alive forever. Use ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@cached_property"}],", or cache a module-level function instead."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Caches need hashable arguments."}]," A ",["$","code",null,{"children":"dict"}]," or ",["$","code",null,{"children":"list"}]," argument raises ",["$","code",null,{"children":"TypeError: unhashable type"}],". The cache is also per-process, not shared."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Forgetting to return."}]," If the wrapper does not ",["$","code",null,{"children":"return func(...)"}],", every decorated call returns ",["$","code",null,{"children":"None"}],". Silent and common."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":["Skipping ",["$","code",null,{"children":"functools.wraps"}]]}]," loses ",["$","code",null,{"children":"__name__"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"__doc__"}],", and annotations, which breaks docs tooling and test discovery. It also sets ",["$","code",null,{"children":"__wrapped__"}],", so ",["$","code",null,{"children":"inspect.signature"}]," sees through the wrapper and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"inspect.unwrap(f)"}]," recovers the original."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"Recursion goes through the wrapper every time."}]," That is what makes ",["$","code",null,{"children":"@cache"}]," turn exponential recursion into linear, and what makes a logging decorator extremely noisy."]}],"\n",["$","li",null,{"children":["Debugging: check ",["$","code",null,{"children":"f.__wrapped__"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"f.__name__"}],", and ",["$","code",null,{"children":"inspect.signature(f)"}]," to confirm what you actually got."]}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","hr",null,{}],"\n",["$","h2",null,{"id":"17-context-managers","children":["17. ",["$","code",null,{"children":"Mapping"}],") and return concrete ones (",["$","code",null,{"children":"list"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"children":"dict"}],"); never annotate a mutable default as ",["$","code",null,{"children":"list"}]," with ",["$","code",null,{"children":"[]"}],"; and remember hints are just metadata, readable at runtime with ",["$","code",null,{"children":"typing.get_type_hints(func)"}],", which is how pydantic and dataclasses work."]}],"\n",["$","p",null,{"children":["Run ",["$","code",null,{"children":"mypy"}]," or
